====================================================================================================
QUESTION: What are the three conditions that must be met to reinstate a lapsed policy?
CATEGORY: CAT3_CONDITIONAL_REASONING
EXPECTED ANSWER: The policy owner must provide evidence of insurability satisfactory to the insurer, pay all overdue premiums with interest at 6% per annum, and repay or reinstate any policy loans with interest.
EVIDENCE: 10.1 You may reinstate this policy within 3 years of lapse if: (a) You provide evidence of insurability satisfactory to us (b) You pay all overdue premiums with interest at 6% per annum (c) You repay or reinstate any policy loans with interest
====================================================================================================

Reinstatement within 3 years of lapse requires payment of overdue premiums with interest, which is a necessary condition for revival.
